09373d18025f1daf2d92dca44f19af4ea4806b9a: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
V &= \frac{nC+mD}{m+n} \\
&= \frac{2C+3D}{3+2} \\
&= \frac{2\vec{c}+3\vec{d}}{5} \\
&= \frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d}
\end{aligned}$$

310f3d37deeeac136c2e001b87770cf99ec0d277: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{UV} &= V-U \\
&= \left( \frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d} \right)-\frac{4}{5}\vec{c} \\
&= \color{#2f9e44}-\frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d}
\end{aligned}$$

9e1a6ebf794590edff60896392f1143c37b82aad: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{DE} &= \vec{E}-\vec{D} = (1+1,2-5,-4-2) = \color{#2f9e44}2i-3j-6k \\
|\vec{DE}| &= \sqrt{ 2^2+(-3)^2+(-6)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}7 \\
\vec{EF} &= \vec{F}-\vec{E} = (3-1,-4-2,-1+4) = \color{#2f9e44}2i-6j+3k \\ \\
|\vec{DE}| &= 7 \\
|\vec{EF}| &= \sqrt{ 2^2+(-6)^2+3^2 } = 7 \\
\end{aligned}$$

9193e6499df6a4d4c625ec8ff9989954536b0567: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
|\vec{PQ}| &= \sqrt{ (5-0)^2+(12-0)^2+(0-0)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}13 \\
|\vec{PR}| &= \sqrt{ (0-0)^2+(8-0)^2+(15-0)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}17 \\
|\vec{QR}| &= \sqrt{ (5-0)^2+(12-8)^2+(0-15)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}\sqrt{ 266 } \\
\frac{1}{|\vec{PQ}|}\vec{PQ} &= \frac{1}{13}(5,12,0) = \color{#2f9e44}\frac{5}{13}i+\frac{12}{13}j
\end{aligned}$$

fbecd7e0b8121467e408a611b32c88921f128e19: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{a}*\vec{b} &= (3*2)+(-6*1)+(2*(-2)) = \color{#2f9e44}-4 \\
comp_{\vec{b}}\vec{a} &= \frac{\vec{a}*\vec{b}}{|\vec{b}|} = \frac{-4}{\sqrt{ 2^2+1^2+(-2)^2 }} = \color{#2f9e44}-\frac{4}{3} \\
proj_{\vec{b}}\vec{a} &= \frac{\vec{a}*\vec{b}}{|\vec{b}|^2}\vec{b} = \frac{-4}{9}(2,1,-2) = \color{#2f9e44}-\frac{8}{9}i-\frac{4}{9}j+\frac{8}{9}k \\
comp_{\vec{a}}\vec{b} &= \frac{\vec{b}*\vec{a}}{|\vec{a}|} = \frac{-4}{\sqrt{ 3^2+(-6)^2+2^2 }} = \color{#2f9e44}-\frac{4}{7}
\end{aligned}$$

ec30f3c42b3bfed302edcd81847245b37308108f: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
|\vec{v}| &= \sqrt{ q^2+(q-1)^2+5^2 } \\
\sqrt{ 38 } &= \sqrt{ q^2+q^2-2q+1+25 } \\
38 &= 2q^2-2q+26 \\
2q^2-2q-12 &= 0 \\
q^2-q-6 &= 0 \\
(q-3)(q+2) &= 0 \\
q &= \color{#2f9e44}3
\end{aligned}$$

cb467119dd62897c08ff5ff53cfb599a49191da8: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{u}*\vec{w} &= (1*x)+(-2*y)+(3*(-3)) \\

x-2y &= 5
\end{aligned}$$

f8ae978742b9c55291aab058e61f0b5c173d5a2c: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{v}*\vec{w} &= (-2*x)+(1*y)+(2*(-3)) \\
-7 &= -2x+y-6 \\
2x-y &= 1
\end{aligned}$$

801645c7b0910f87dee53b3611ed161ebd5a541b: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
